- **Summary**: 该论文利用大模型生成的合成数据微调双语编码器和交叉编码器，构建了非英语临床编码检索系统。在多个语言上提升了召回率，MRR提升15.9%。

<h3 id="71">[71] Seeing Isn't Knowing: Do VLMs Know When Not to Answer Spatial Questions (and Why)?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30557](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30557) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yue Zhang, Zun Wang, Han Lin, Yonatan Bitton, Idan Szpektor, Mohit Bansal
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Visual Reasoning, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Summary**: 该论文构建了SpatialUncertain框架，测试VLMs在遮挡和视角歧义下的空间推理能力。发现模型过度自信，准确率低，且无法识别可靠视角。

<h3 id="72">[72] VLM3: Vision Language Models Are Native 3D Learners</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30561](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30561) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhipeng Cai, Zhuang Liu, Yunyang Xiong, Zechun Liu, Vikas Chandra, Yangyang Shi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, 3D Vision, Depth Estimation
- **Summary**: 该论文提出VLM3，证明标准VLM通过焦距统一、文本像素参考和数据缩放即可有效进行3D学习，在深度估计和3D任务上达到专家模型精度。

<h3 id="73">[73] Memory-Bound but Not Bandwidth-Limited: The Physical AI Inference Gap in Batch-1 LLM Decode</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30571](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30571) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Josef Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AR; cs.AI; cs.DC; cs.PF; cs.RO
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, GPU Computing, Memory Efficiency
- **Summary**: 该论文测量了不同GPU上批量大小为1的LLM解码延迟，发现峰值带宽利用率低，启动开销显著。CUDA Graphs在H100上提升1.259倍。

<h3 id="74">[74] Prior Availability in Industrial Visual Sim-to-Real: A Review of CAD-Guided and CAD-Unavailable Regimes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30581](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30581) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chenxi Tao, Seung-Kyum Choi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Sim-to-Real, Domain Adaptation, Industrial AI
- **Summary**: 该综述按先验可用性分类工业视觉仿真到现实转换，分别讨论CAD可用、不可用和边界先验场景。实证表明渲染数量不足以弥合差距，测试时CAD可提供验证通道。

<h3 id="75">[75] Benchmarking Machine Learning Uncertainty Quantification Methodologies for Predicting Turbine Gas Temperature Degradation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30585](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30585) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jostein Barry-Straume, Changmin Son, Adrian Sandu, Gavan Burke, Rekha Sundararajan, Andrew Rimell, James G. Steinrock
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CE
- **Tags**: Uncertainty Estimation, Predictive Maintenance, Prognostics and Health Management
- **Summary**: 该论文比较了五种预测区间构建方法用于涡轮燃气温度预测，通过覆盖率、宽度和稳定性评估，展示了不同方法的权衡。

<h3 id="76">[76] ImmigrationQA: A Source-Grounded Dataset and Small-Model Adaptation for U.S. Immigration Law</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30589](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30589) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nazarii Shportun
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Legal AI, Question Answering, Dataset
- **Summary**: 该论文构建了ImmigrationQA数据集，包含17,058个美国移民法律问答对，并微调了Llama 3.2 3B模型。微调后模型得分相对提升27%，在程序性子领域表现更好。

<h3 id="77">[77] Counterfactual Evaluation Reveals Hidden Capability Profiles in Clinical LLMs and Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30590](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30590) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Matt Turk
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Medical AI, Causal Reasoning
- **Venue**: RLEval @ ACM CAIS 2026 (Workshop on Methods and RL Environments for Evaluating AI Agents)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出因果敏感性分数（CSS），通过干预临床案例维度评估LLM的响应性。发现覆盖度指标无法暴露模型缺陷，存在手术状态盲点。

<h3 id="78">[78] Scientific Machine Learning for Engine Health Management and Remaining Useful Life Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30593](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30593) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jostein Barry-Straume, Changmin Son, Adrian Sandu, Gavan Burke, Rekha Sundararajan, Andrew Rimell, James G. Steinrock
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CE
- **Tags**: Prognostics and Health Management, Multi-Task Learning, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一个多任务科学机器学习框架，联合预测涡轮燃气温度和剩余使用寿命，并量化不确定性。在多个指标上评估了预测性能。

<h3 id="79">[79] An Organization-Scoped LLM Agent Runtime Architecture for Regulated Cybersecurity Operations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30604](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30604) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: George Fatouros, Georgios Makridis, George Kousiouris, John Soldatos, Dimosthenis Kyriazis
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.IR
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Cybersecurity, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 该论文提出一种组织范围的LLM智能体运行时架构，适用于受监管的网络安全操作。通过类型化安全上下文和治理工具适配层，实现可审计、模型无关的部署。

<h3 id="80">[80] Crafter: A Multi-Agent Harness for Editable Scientific Figure Generation from Diverse Inputs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30611](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30611) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haozhe Zhao, Shuzheng Si, Zhenhailong Wang, Zheng Wang, Liang Chen, Xiaotong Li, Zhixiang Liang, Maosong Sun, Minjia Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Image Generation, Data Visualization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/HaozheZhao/Crafter)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出Crafter多智能体框架，用于从多种输入生成可编辑的科学图表。在CraftBench基准上优于现有方法，并支持输出可编辑SVG。

<h3 id="81">[81] Reward Learning from Best-of-$N$ Preference Data: Targets, Tradeoffs, and Design Principles</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30619](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30619) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rattana Pukdee, Maria-Florina Balcan, Pradeep Ravikumar
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, RLHF
- **Summary**: 该论文研究了从Best-of-N采样构建的偏好对中学习Bradley-Terry奖励函数的问题。通过分析偏好数据的条件分布，推导了独立参考变体的封闭式奖励目标，并揭示了Best-vs-Random等变体存在非BT可表示性问题。实验表明，N的大小与数据收集瓶颈有关，并提供了设计原则。

<h3 id="82">[82] Active Timepoint Selection for Learning Measure-Valued Trajectories</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30625](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30625) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nicolas Huynh, Mihaela van der Schaar
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Active Learning, Gaussian Process, Optimal Transport
- **Summary**: 该论文针对单细胞生物学等场景中从稀疏快照推断连续概率路径的问题，提出了一种主动学习策略。通过线性化最优传输将分布快照映射到适于高斯过程建模的切空间，从而构建概率路径的代理模型，并迭代选择测量时间点以最小化不确定性。实验证明该方法优于不确定性不可知的基线。

<h3 id="83">[83] The Architecture of Errors: From Universal Impossibility to Patch-Local LLM Reliability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30628](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30628) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mikhail L. Arbuzov, Lee Mosbacker, Sisong Bei, Ziwei Dong, Dmitri Kalaev, Alexey Shvets
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Formal Methods
- **Summary**: 该论文论证了通用LLM可靠性不可能通过有限干预词典实现，但在操作受限的局部补丁中，错误稀疏且重复，可靠性成为局部目录发现和干预覆盖问题。通过两个命题和一个推论形式化了这种转变，将困难从指数级转移到长序列的硬决策数量上。

<h3 id="84">[84] Controllable Lung Nodule Synthesis via Histogram-Regularized Latent Diffusion Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30631](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30631) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arunkumar Kannan, Yanbo Zhang, Han Liu, Michael Baumgartner, Jianing Wang, Alexander Hertel, Bogdan Georgescu, Sasa Grbic
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Diffusion Model, Image Synthesis
- **Summary**: 该论文针对CT肺结节数据稀缺问题，提出了一种可控潜在扩散模型，通过直方图正则化项约束体素强度分布，生成不同亚型（实性、部分实性、磨玻璃）的肺结节。实验证明合成结节在视觉真实感和下游任务中表现良好，能改善不常见亚型的分类性能。

<h3 id="85">[85] Rationalize: Shared Semantic Reasoning for Human-AI Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30632](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30632) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aritra Dasgupta, Naga Datha Saikiran Battula, Avina Nakarmi, Sohom Sen, Subhodeep Ghosh, Xun Song
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Human-AI Collaboration, LLM Alignment
- **Venue**: ACM CHI 2026 BiAlign Workshop
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了Rationalize框架，通过角色对（如探索者-引导者等）实现人类与AI模型在数据驱动意义构建中的共享语义推理。该框架在共享推理空间中使意图和行动合理化，促进双向对齐，并提出了对齐设计和评估的研究议程。

<h3 id="86">[86] Score Broadcast and Decorrelation: A General Framework for Broadcast-Based Credit Assignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30638](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30638) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mustafa Uzun, Mete Erdogan, Cengiz Pehlevan, Alper T. Erdogan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neuroscience, Deep Learning Theory, Credit Assignment
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了Score Broadcast and Decorrelation (SBD)框架，将误差广播推广到一般可微损失函数。通过引入输出分数与隐藏层激活之间的正交性原理，统一了交叉熵、Bregman散度等损失族的信用分配，并提供了神经科学中三因子学习规则的理论基础。实验表明SBD在CIFAR-10和Tiny ImageNet上优于现有广播方法。

<h3 id="87">[87] PInVerify: An Offline Embodied Benchmark for Active Instance Verification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30639](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30639) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuhang Jiang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Vision-Language Model, Benchmark
- **Venue**: Foundation Models Meet Embodied Agents (FMEA) Workshop, CVPR 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Avalon-S/PInVerify)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了主动实例验证任务，要求智能体通过主动选择视角来验证候选对象是否匹配细粒度语言描述。发布了PInVerify基准，包含3000个评估片段和多种基线，评估了多种多模态大模型和嵌入模型，发现主动视角选择未带来可靠增益。

<h3 id="88">[88] COFT: Counterfactual-Conformal Decoding for Fair Chain-of-Thought Reasoning in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30641](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30641) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arya Fayyazi, Mehdi Kamal, Massoud Pedram
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Fairness, LLM Alignment
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了COFT方法，一种无需训练的解码方法，通过反事实掩码和分裂符合校准，在解码时实现标记级公平性控制，并提供分布自由的有效性保证。在多个模型和偏见基准上，COFT将偏见指标降低30-55%，且不影响任务效用。

<h3 id="89">[89] Same Patient, Different Words, Different Diagnosis? Evaluating Semantic Stability in Clinical LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30646](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30646) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mahdi Alkaeed, Adnan Qayyum, Nabeel Abo Kashreef, Muhammad Bilal, Junaid Qadir
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, LLM Evaluation, Robustness Evaluation
- **Summary**: 该论文评估了临床LLM对语义等价但措辞不同的提示的稳定性，提出了基于自然语言推理的语义验证框架和三个敏感性指标。实验发现领域专用模型并不一致地更鲁棒，通用基础模型也有竞争力。

<h3 id="90">[90] LARK: Learnability-Grounded Trajectory Selection for Efficient Reasoning Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30651](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30651) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tianrun Yu, Kaixiang Zhao, Chih-Chun Chen, Amanda Hughes, Taylor W. Killian, Fenglong Ma, Weitong Zhang, Porter Jenkins
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, LLM Reasoning, Data Selection
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Tianrun-Yu/LARK)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了LARK方法，基于可学习性选择推理轨迹用于蒸馏，通过可学习性因子ρ和χ²正则化策略平衡可学习性和分布覆盖。实验表明LARK在多个基模型和推理任务上优于基线，且所选轨迹能更快降低监督微调损失。

<h3 id="91">[91] EUDAIMONIA: Evaluating Undesirable Dynamics in AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30654](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30654) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jun Rui Huang, Wang Bill Zhu, Ziyi Liu, Nathanael Fast, Ravi Iyer, Robin Jia
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: AI Safety, LLM Safety, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了社会AI设计规范，用于评估LLM在社交互动中是否与用户福祉一致，包括有害亲密、依赖等风险。构建了EUDAIMONIA基准，发现即使最强模型也违反30.7%的检查，扩展思考未减少违规率。

<h3 id="92">[92] Automatically Attacking Software Reverse Engineering AI Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30667](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30667) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Brian Crawford, Justin Phillips, Patrick McClure
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Prompt Injection, LLM Security, Reverse Engineering
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一种基于遗传算法生成提示注入的攻击技术，通过修改二进制可执行文件中的变量赋值，欺骗LLM驱动的反汇编和反编译系统，使之误解代码。证明了整合LLM的网络安全工具链存在新的安全漏洞。

<h3 id="93">[93] CobSeg: Coherence Boundary Modeling for Dialogue Topic Segmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30668](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30668) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sijin Sun, Liangbin Zhao, Jiaxiang Cai, Ming Deng, Mingyu Luo, Xiuju Fu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dialogue System, Discourse Segmentation, Natural Language Understanding
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了CobSeg，一种多分支架构，分离连贯性级语义连续性和词法边界转换，通过方向性边界预测和边界信息加权进行对话主题分割。在多个基准上提升了P_k和W_d指标，尤其在局部词法线索显著时。

<h3 id="94">[94] Human-Alignment, Calibration, and Activation Patterns in Large Language Model Uncertainty</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30675](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30675) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kyle Moore, Jesse Roberts, Daryl Watson, William Ward, Grayson Heyboer
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Uncertainty Estimation, LLM Alignment
- **Summary**: 该论文研究了LLM不确定性是否与人类不确定性相似，即不确定性对齐。在多选题和开放式事实回忆数据集上评估了校准和对齐，并刻画了指令微调的影响。

<h3 id="95">[95] Investigating Detection and Obfuscation of Prompt Injection Attacks Against Software Reverse Engineering AI Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30677](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30677) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Brian Crawford, Patrick McClure
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Prompt Injection, LLM Security, Reverse Engineering
- **Summary**: 该论文针对软件逆向工程智能体中的提示注入攻击，演示了检测攻击字符串的防御策略，并探索了混淆和反混淆方法。增进了对智能体软件分析系统安全性的理解。

<h3 id="96">[96] How Early Adopters Used Generative AI Worldwide: Variation by Country Income and Language</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30685](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30685) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Madeleine I. G. Daepp, Isaac Slaughter
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.HC
- **Tags**: LLM Application, Multilingual Learning, AI and Labor
- **Summary**: 该论文通过大规模匿名交互数据集，分析了不同国家早期用户生成式AI使用方式的差异。发现教育是多数国家最常见用途，且与GDP负相关；休闲用途与收入正相关；语言差异影响使用模式。

<h3 id="97">[97] Depth-Dependent Indirect Prompt Injection in Tool-Calling ReAct Agents: Injection Depth, Payload Framing, and Turn-Budget Sensitivity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30686](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30686) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohammadreza Rashidi
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Prompt Injection, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 现有基准在固定条件下评估间接提示注入攻击成功率，忽略了注入位置、框架和轮次预算的影响。本文通过四项对照实验系统研究了这些维度，发现注入深度是主导变量，GPT-4o-mini的ASR随深度从60%衰减至0%，而Claude Haiku在所有深度均为0%。研究还发现框架和轮次预算影响有限。

<h3 id="98">[98] ConTrans: Learning Text-enhanced Local-global Temporal Representations for Zero-shot Temporal Action Localization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30689](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30689) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kanchan Keisham, Thenukan Pathmanathan, Thangarajah Akilan
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Zero-Shot Learning, Video Understanding
- **Summary**: 零样本时序动作定位（ZS-TAL）现有方法主要建模长程上下文，忽略了帧间局部相关性，且网络结构浅层导致特征表示能力受限。本文提出ConTrans，融合卷积归纳偏置与Transformer自注意力以联合捕获细粒度局部依赖和长程全局上下文。在ActivityNet-1.3和THUMOS14数据集上显著优于现有方法，建立了新基准。

<h3 id="99">[99] Seeing Before Agreeing: Aligning Multi-Agent Consensus with Visual Evidence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30698](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30698) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuhan Wang, Shuochen Chang, Yalin Feng, Dongsheng Ma, Yuanzi Li, Zhengren Wang, Yinglong Yang, Yufei Chen, Yikang Wang, Shaoxu Sun, Wentao Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Multi-Agent System, Visual Reasoning
- **Summary**: 多智能体视觉问答中现有方法主要关注文本讨论，忽略了视觉证据的对齐。本文揭示答案级共识不可靠，需要对齐视觉证据，提出EAGLE框架，显式暴露每个智能体的视觉定位区域并基于证据一致性进行决策。在六个VQA基准上取得最佳平均性能。

<h3 id="100">[100] SAGE: A Novelty Gate for Efficient Memory Evolution in Agentic LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30711](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30711) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sijia Wang, Dhanajit Brahma, Ricardo Henao
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG; stat.ML
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture, Novelty Detection
- **Summary**: 代理LLM需要决定新事实是否应加入记忆，但现有工作侧重检索和存储，缺乏原则性写入控制。本文将记忆演化视为新颖性检测问题，提出SAGE，基于von Mises-Fisher密度估计和自适应阈值实现新颖性感知写入控制。在LoCoMo上取得最佳平均token-F1，同时减少API成本和延迟。

<h3 id="101">[101] Simple Token-Efficient Vision-Language Model for Case-level Pathology Synoptic Report Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30716](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30716) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhiyuan Yang, Jiahao Cheng, Vincent Quoc-Huy Trinh, Mahdi S. Hosseini
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Vision-Language Model
- **Venue**: DeLTA 2026
- **Summary**: 从全切片图像生成病理报告面临分辨率高、视觉标记序列长、病例级别推理复杂等挑战。本文提出一个简单的三组件视觉语言模型，使用冻结病理编码器、轻量MLP对齐器和LLM解码器，并采用5倍放大率下的512×512块以减少序列长度。在GPU内存受限下实现高效训练，并在ROUGE-L等指标上取得高分数。

<h3 id="102">[102] When are LLMs Sufficient Policy Optimizers for Sequential RL Tasks?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30719](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30719) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Stephane Hatgis-Kessell, Emma Brunskill
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning, In-Context Learning
- **Summary**: 本文研究LLM能否作为强化学习任务的黑盒策略优化器，提出Prompted Policy Optimization（PromptPO）方法，使用Python描述状态空间、动作空间和奖励函数，让LLM基于交互反馈生成和改进策略。在硬探索环境和真实控制任务上，PromptPO匹配或超越标准RL基线，但在MuJoCo领域表现不佳。

<h3 id="103">[103] Kalimati Vegetable Price Index Forecasting with a Momentum Corrected Online Stacking Ensemble</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30720](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30720) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sahaj Raj Malla
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; econ.GN; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Ensemble Learning, Agricultural AI
- **Summary**: 新兴经济体的农产品价格预测因波动性大、供应中断和节日影响而困难。本文提出Kalimati蔬菜价格指数（KVPI）和动量校正在线堆叠集成模型，在90天预测期实现RMSE=1.771、MAPE=0.68%、R²=0.845。

<h3 id="104">[104] OrcaRouter: A Production-Oriented LLM Router with Hybrid Offline-Online Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30736](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30736) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhenghua Bao, Fengya Tian, Chris Zhang, Zhenjun Chen, Xile Ma, Yi Shi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Routing, LLM Inference, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 面对多个LLM的不同能力和推理成本，需要路由选择。本文提出OrcaRouter，结合LinUCB上下文强盗和混合离线-在线学习，在RouterArena上排名第二，准确率75.54%，成本每千查询1美元。

<h3 id="105">[105] GSAM: A Generalizable and Safe Robotic Framework for Articulated Object Manipulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30740](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30740) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Beichen Shao, Mengying Xie, Heng Su, Wanyi Zhang, Mingyan Li, Yan Ding, Fausto Giunchiglia, Chao Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robotics, Vision-Language Model, Safe RL
- **Venue**: 19th International Conference on Parallel Problem Solving from Nature (PPSN 2026)
- **Summary**: 关节物体操作面临泛化有限和碰撞问题。本文提出GSAM框架，结合视觉感知器、VLM精炼器（使用COT常识推理）和交互约束函数生成器，实现安全操作。在5类50种铰链任务上，标准差降低3.1%，操作成功率提升36.0%。

<h3 id="106">[106] Chatterbox-Flash: Prior-Calibrated Block Diffusion for Streaming Zero-Shot TTS</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30748](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30748) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Deokjin Seo, Gangin Park, Kihyun Nam
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Speech Synthesis, Zero-Shot Learning, Diffusion Model
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/resemble-ai/chatterbox-flash)
- **Summary**: 零样本文本转语音模型在将块扩散解码应用于离散语音标记时存在长尾分布偏差。本文提出先验校准评分和早期解码调度，无需架构修改即可缓解问题。在标准基准上达到与强基线相当的高保真合成，同时支持流式推理。

<h3 id="107">[107] XLGoBench: Detecting cross-lingual skill gaps with algorithmic tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30788](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30788) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Purvam Jain, Preethi Jyothi, Vihari Piratla, Suvrat Raju
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, Multilingual Learning
- **Summary**: 跨语言能力差距检测缺乏可扩展、可量化且透明的基准。本文提出XLGoBench，一组合成算法任务，具有跨语言可比性、可扩展性、可量化和透明性。通过实验揭示了多个最先进模型中的持续跨语言差距。

<h3 id="108">[108] Smaller Models are Natural Explorers for Policy-Level Diversity in GRPO</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30789](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30789) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yiming Ren, Yiran Xu, Zicheng Lin, Chufan Shi, Yukang Chen, Dingdong Wang, Tianhe Wu, Junjie Wang, Yujiu Yang, Yu Qiao, Ruihang Chu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Reinforcement Learning, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: GRPO需要多样化的rollout，但现有方法主要增加token级随机性，可能导致噪声。本文发现小模型天然具有更高的策略级多样性，提出S2L-PO框架，利用小模型作为探索者训练大模型，并设计渐进退火策略。在数学推理基准上提升8.8%且减少rollout计算。

<h3 id="109">[109] On the impact of retrieved content representations in RAG Pipelines</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30790](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30790) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jonathan J Ross, Bevan Koopman, Anton van der Vegt, Guido Zuccon
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: RAG, Information Retrieval, Question Answering
- **Summary**: RAG管道继承为人类设计的检索组件，但内容表示对LLM影响未知。本文通过控制实验比较14种表示变换，发现答案保留（answer retention）是生成准确性的主要决定因素，而措辞、结构、长度和查询依赖性影响有限。

<h3 id="110">[110] OpenSTBench: Beyond Semantic Evaluation for Speech Translation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30792](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30792) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yanjie An, Yuxiang Zhao, Yichi Zhang, Qixi Zheng, Yujie Tu, Keqi Deng, Kai Yu, Xie Chen
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Speech Translation, LLM Evaluation, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/sjtuayj/OpenSTBench)
- **Summary**: 语音翻译系统评估在翻译质量、语音质量和时间质量方面缺乏统一框架。本文提出OpenSTBench，一个统一的多维评估框架，支持S2TT和S2ST系统在离线和流式设置下的联合评估。实验显示不同系统在这些维度上存在显著差异。

<h3 id="111">[111] MechVQA: Benchmarking and Enhancing Multimodal LLMs on Comprehensive Mechanical Drawing Understanding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30794](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30794) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qian Kou, Xiaofeng Shi, Yulin Li, Xiaosong Qiu, Xinyang Wang, Hua Zhou, Cao Dongxing
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Document Understanding, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 多模态大模型在机械工程图纸理解上表现脆弱，由于高标注密度和弱领域知识。本文提出MechVQA数据集和MechVL模型，通过多阶段训练建立领域专用基线。MechVL在总分上超过最强闭源基线7.57个百分点。

<h3 id="112">[112] Design and Evaluation of Multi-Agent AI Oracle Systems for Prediction Market Resolution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30802](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30802) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tarun Kota
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Prediction Market, Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 预测市场依赖可靠的结果裁决，现有系统在快速自动化和准确人工仲裁间权衡。本文评估多智能体LLM架构，发现独立聚合（置信度加权投票）达到83.43%准确率，超过最佳单模型1.01%；协商共识反而降低。提出混合AI-人工系统准则。

<h3 id="113">[113] Differentially Private Preference Data Synthesis for Large Language Model Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30808](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30808) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fengyu Gao, Jing Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Differential Privacy
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/gfengyu/Differentially-Private-Preference-Data-Synthesis)
- **Summary**: 针对大语言模型偏好对齐中真实人类偏好数据的隐私问题，提出DPPrefSyn算法，基于Bradley-Terry模型和差分隐私主成分分析生成合成偏好数据。实验证明在强差分隐私保证下实现有竞争力的对齐性能。这是首项生成差分隐私合成偏好数据的工作。

<h3 id="114">[114] GaMi: Geometry-Agnostic Material Identification via Cross-Modal Subtractive Disentanglement</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30818](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30818) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhiwei Chen, Yijie Li, Yimo Zhang, Shiyun Shao, Yichao Chen, Dian Ding, Liang Wang, Haiwei Wu, Liwei Guo, Jie Yang, Xiaosong Zhang, Yongzhao Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.ET; cs.AI; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Sensor Fusion
- **Summary**: 为解决非接触式材料识别中几何变化和单模态模糊性问题，提出GaMi多模态系统，融合毫米波和声学传感，通过跨模态减法解耦提取材料特征。在20种材料上达到95.2%准确率，超越单模态基线。

<h3 id="115">[115] Unlearning in Diffusion Models: A Unified Framework with KL Divergence and Likelihood Constraints</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30825](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30825) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shervin Khalafi, Alejandro Ribeiro, Dongsheng Ding
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.OC; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Machine Unlearning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对扩散模型去学习中保留模型效用与移除不良数据/概念的矛盾，提出基于KL散度和似然约束的约束优化框架。理论证明强对偶性，实验显示优于基于权重的基线方法，实现更好的保留-去学习权衡。

<h3 id="116">[116] Beyond Agreement: Scoring Panel-Surfaced Biomedical Entity Candidates for Curator Triage</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30826](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30826) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shuheng Cao, Ruiqi Chen, Renjie Cao, Zhenhao Zhang, Siyu Zhang, Tingting Dan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Named Entity Recognition, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 针对生物医学命名实体识别中多LLM一致不代表语料惯例正确性的问题，提出BioConCal评分器，利用协议、提及等特征对候选实体排序。在五个数据集上将AUROC从0.753提升至0.910，在0.95精度目标下选中1340个候选，而原始协议仅293个。

<h3 id="117">[117] Your Teacher Can't Help You Here: Combating Supervision Fidelity Decay in On-Policy Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30833](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30833) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yanjiang Liu, Jie Lou, Xinyan Guan, Yuqiu Ji, Hongyu Lin, Ben He, Xianpei Han, Le Sun, Xing Yu, Yaojie Lu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, LLM Reasoning, Code Generation
- **Summary**: 指出在线蒸馏中学生生成前缀变长时教师监督保真度下降的问题，提出Lookahead Group Reward，基于教师对后续步骤的置信度分配奖励。在数学和代码基准上，7B学生模型平均提升2.57个点，长生成任务提升4.92点。

<h3 id="118">[118] Hide-and-Seek in Trajectories: Discovering Failure Signals for VLA Runtime Monitoring</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30834](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30834) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Seongheon Park, Wendi Li, Changdae Oh, Samuel Yeh, Zsolt Kira, Michael Hagenow, Sharon Li
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Failure Detection, Robotics
- **Summary**: 针对视觉-语言-行动模型执行失败检测需昂贵重采样或外部分类器的问题，提出Hide-and-Seek框架，通过轨迹间和轨迹内对比学习从粗粒度标签定位失败动作。在LIBERO、VLABench和真实机器人上实现最先进的多任务失败检测性能。

<h3 id="119">[119] Fine-Tuning Improves Information Conveyance in Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30844](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30844) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuwei Cheng, Weiyi Tian, Haifeng Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/WeiyiTian/canopy-entropy)
- **Summary**: 针对现有微调不确定性分析忽略生成长度混淆因素的问题，提出Canopy Entropy (CE*)度量，联合捕获输出长度和序列熵。发现微调模型在总熵降低时，长度与熵率的正相关性增强，表明不确定性被重组为更有信息量的生成。

<h3 id="120">[120] Safe Equilibrium Policy Optimization for Strategic Agent Policies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30854](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30854) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Karthika Arumugam, Kiran Kumar Manku, Amit Dhanda
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Multi-Agent System, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 针对语言模型强化学习忽视多智能体战略结构导致有害均衡的问题，提出SEPO目标函数，在期望回报中加入可剥削性、共谋风险和外部性惩罚。在五个战略域中实现零可剥削优势，纠正过度合作行为。

<h3 id="121">[121] DARTS: Distribution-Aware Active Rollout Trajectory Shaping for Accelerating LLM Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30859](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30859) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yujie Wang, Siwei Chen, Longzan Luo, Xinyi Liu, Xupeng Miao, Fangcheng Fu, Bin Cui
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Reinforcement Learning, Training Efficiency
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对LLM强化学习中生成长尾分布导致效率瓶颈的问题，提出DARTS方法，通过主动分布塑形和感知分布的轨迹采样加速训练。在不损失性能下实现最高1.77倍加速。

<h3 id="122">[122] Sophrosyne: Agentic Exploration of Relational Data Systems Needs Moderation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30862](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30862) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Madhav Jivrajani, Ramnatthan Alagappan, Aishwarya Ganesan
- **Subjects**: cs.DB; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-SQL, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 针对Text2SQL智能体过度探索数据库模式导致查询不准确的问题，提出Sophrosyne环境，在API响应中添加指令引导探索。减少4.6倍过度探索，精度提升12.4%。

<h3 id="123">[123] Federated Variational Preference Alignment with Gumbel-Softmax Prior for Personalized User Preferences</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30873](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30873) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jabin Koo, Hoyoung Kim, Minwoo Jang, Jungseul Ok
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, LLM Alignment, LLM Personalization
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对联邦学习中变分偏好学习因本地数据稀缺导致后验坍塌的问题，提出FedVPA-GP框架，使用联邦混合先验和正交损失解耦偏好。在HH-RLHF数据集上显著优于单体基线，成功分离冲突的用户意图。

<h3 id="124">[124] PatchWorld: Gradient-Free Optimization of Executable World Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30880](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30880) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiaxin Bai, Yue Guo, Yifei Dong, Jiaxuan Xiong, Tianshi Zheng, Yixia Li, Tianqing Fang, Yufei Li, Yisen Gao, Haoyu Huang, Zhongwei Xie, Hong Ting Tsang, Zihao Wang, Lihui Liu, Jeff Pan, Yangqiu Song
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Code Generation, World Model
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/HKBU-KnowComp/PatchWorld)
- **Summary**: 针对文本智能体环境通常建模为POMDP但缺乏可执行世界模型的问题，提出PatchWorld框架，通过反例引导代码修复从离线轨迹生成可执行Python世界模型。在七个环境上，不调用LLM即可实现76.4%宏观成功率。

<h3 id="125">[125] A Unified and Reproducible Experimentation Framework for Speech Understanding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30899](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30899) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jing Peng, Junhao Du, Chenghao Wang, Hanqi Li, Yi Yang, Yixuan Wang, Xiaoyu Gu, Guanyu Chen, Yucheng Wang, Jiang Li, Zhangjie Zhao, Haoran Wang, Wenming Tu, Haoyu Li, Duo Ma, Lirong Qian, Yu Xi, Wen Wen, Jiaqi Guo, Hui Zhang, Shuai Fan, Wenbin Jiang, Shuai Wang, Kai Yu
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Speech Processing, Benchmark, Research Reproducibility
- **Summary**: 针对语音基础模型评估不一致和训练难复现的问题，提出SURE统一实验框架，标准化预测格式、归一化和评分。涵盖从传统流水线到语音LLM，提高可比较性和可复现性。

<h3 id="126">[126] Inverse Reinforcement Learning without an Optimal Demonstrator: A Feasible Reward Set Approach</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30903](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30903) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kihyun Kim, Shripad Deshmukh, Nikos Vlassis, Jiawei Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Inverse Reinforcement Learning, RLHF
- **Summary**: 针对逆强化学习假设单一最优示范者但实际数据来自多个非最优者的问题，提出可行奖励集框架，编码各示范者的次优性约束。理论分析给出恢复保证，在网格世界和LLM微调中展现有效性。

<h3 id="127">[127] BlueFin: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Financial Spreadsheets</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30907](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30907) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Srivatsa Kundurthy, Clara Na, Colton Moraine, Anoushka Mohta, Case Winter, George Fang, John Ling, Emma Strubell, Zach Kirshner
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Benchmark, Financial AI
- **Summary**: 针对财务领域电子表格任务缺乏LLM智能体基准的问题，提出BlueFin基准，包含131个复杂任务和3225个评判标准。前沿LLM平均得分低于50%，在动态正确性上表现薄弱。

<h3 id="128">[128] What Makes LVLMs Hallucinate Less? Unveiling the Architectural Factors Behind Hallucination Robustness</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30911](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30911) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yusheng He, Jizhe Zhou, Xia Du, Zheng Lin, Jun Luo, Jiancheng Lv
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, LLM Hallucination, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 针对大型视觉语言模型幻觉的架构因素缺乏系统研究的问题，提出CoSimUE基准，通过受控扰动映射设计选择与幻觉行为。发现参数扩展影响有限，视觉编码器和对齐策略分别缓解相似性和不确定性幻觉。

<h3 id="129">[129] Toxic HallucinAItions: Perturbing Prompts and Tracing LLM Circuits</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30913](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30913) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Soorya Ram Shimgekar, Agam Goyal, Amruta Parulekar, Joshua Chen, Yian Wang, Navin Kumar, Hari Sundaram, Eshwar Chandrasekharan, Koustuv Saha
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.HC
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, LLM Hallucination, Prompt Engineering
- **Summary**: 该论文研究了提示中的毒性语言如何影响大语言模型的事实可靠性。通过控制提示的词汇和语气变化（礼貌、随机、三种毒性程度），在ARC-Easy、GSM8K和MMLU上评估了五个LLM，发现毒性扰动一致地降低了事实准确性并增加了不确定性，而礼貌措辞影响有限。归因图分析表明，毒性放大了对扰动敏感的变体节点，而相对稳定的核心推理节点保持不变。

<h3 id="130">[130] De-attribute to Forget for LLM Unlearning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30919](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30919) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinyang Lu, Jiabao Pan, Rachael Hwee Ling Sim, See-Kiong Ng, Anthony Kum Hoe Tung, Bryan Kian Hsiang Low
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Machine Unlearning, LLM Safety
- **Summary**: 现有的大语言模型遗忘方法通常优化预测损失（如最大化遗忘集损失），但容易导致过度遗忘和模型效用下降。本文提出DareU，一个基于数据属性奖励的强化学习框架，通过减少生成响应与遗忘数据的属性得分来实现遗忘。实验表明，DareU在平衡遗忘质量和模型效用方面优于现有基线。

<h3 id="131">[131] TUX: Measuring Human--AI Tacit Understanding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30930](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30930) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yueshen Li, Hanyi Min, Vedant Das Swain, Koustuv Saha
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Human-Computer Interaction, LLM Alignment, Cognitive Science
- **Summary**: 在人机协作中，隐性理解（无需明确沟通的调谐）是重要但未被充分研究的能力。本文设计了一个基于Wavelength游戏的频谱放置任务，定义了隐性理解指数（TUX），并通过241名人类和200个LLM代理进行评估。发现人格特征相近的人机对具有显著更高的TUX，且个体特征、决策风格和信心能更好地解释TUX。

<h3 id="132">[132] Do Large Language Models Encode Institutional Experience? Evidence from Cross-Linguistic Moral Reasoning Under Ambiguity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30934](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30934) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nattavudh Powdthavee
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Cross-Lingual, AI Ethics
- **Summary**: 大语言模型在不同语言中表现出系统性的道德推理差异，但其来源尚不明确。本文假设语言编码了制度环境信息，通过9种语言、6个前沿LLM和两个预注册研究，在制度模糊情境下发现跨语言道德分歧增加，并与现实制度差异相关，而明确制度框架会抑制这种差异。

<h3 id="133">[133] AMix-2: Establishing Protein as a Native Modality in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30963](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30963) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Keyue Qiu, Yixin Wu, Lihao Wang, Yawen Ouyang, Jixiang Yu, Zihan Zhou, Changze Lv, Dongyu Xue, Yuxuan Song, Xinbo Zhang, Hao Wang, Jiangtao Feng, Zhiqiang Gao, Lijun Wu, Xiaoqing Zheng, Ka-Chun Wong, Lei Bai, Ya-Qin Zhang, Wei-Ying Ma, Dahua Lin, Bowen Zhou, Hao Zhou
- **Subjects**: q-bio.BM; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Protein Engineering, Foundation Model, Molecular Generation
- **Summary**: 现有的蛋白质理解与设计通常依赖分离的模型。本文提出AMix-2，一个蛋白质文本基础模型，通过共享词表空间和块级扩散语言建模主干，统一了蛋白质理解和设计。在提出的ProteinArena基准上，AMix-2优于前沿LLM，并与任务专用模型竞争力相当，且扩散范式优于自回归范式。

<h3 id="134">[134] ImmersiveTTS: Environment-Aware Text-to-Speech with Multimodal Diffusion Transformer and Domain-Specific Representation Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30965](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30965) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jun-Hak Yun, Seung-Bin Kim, Seong-Whan Lee
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Speech Synthesis, Audio Generation, Multimodal Learning
- **Venue**: ACL 2026 main conference
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/jjunak-yun/ImmersiveTTS)
- **Summary**: 联合生成与背景环境融合的语音面临声学模式差异的挑战。本文提出ImmersiveTTS，一个环境感知的文本转语音模型，基于多模态扩散Transformer和域特定表示对齐，通过联合注意力融合转录对齐的语音潜变量与环境上下文。实验表明其在自然度、可理解性和音频保真度上优于现有方法。

<h3 id="135">[135] Reading Between the Citations: A Typed Claim Network for Scientific Literature</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30966](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30966) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ning Ding, Sergio J. Rodríguez Méndez, Pouya G. Omran
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Information Extraction, Citation Analysis
- **Summary**: 现有知识图谱仅编码文档间的引用拓扑，丢失了引用的立场信息。本文提出带类型的声明网络，将跨文档引用具体化为带立场标签的声明，并在127篇3D点云语义分割论文上构建了8260个声明的网络。实验表明，该网络在检索增强、聚合立场总结和拓扑分析等任务上优于标准RAG基线。

<h3 id="136">[136] Variational Adapter for Cross-modal Similarity Representation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30968](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30968) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: WenZhang Wei, Zhipeng Gui, Dehua Peng, Tiandi Ye, Huayi Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Multimodal Learning, Representation Learning
- **Venue**: 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML 2026)
- **Summary**: 图像文本匹配数据集缺乏细粒度标注，迫使连续相似度空间压缩为二分类边界，造成假阴性样本。本文提出VACSR，将图像文本匹配重新形式化为变分推理问题，构建相似度潜在空间并通过正则化缓解对二值标注的过拟合。在图像文本检索、域泛化和基础到新类泛化任务中验证了其有效性。

<h3 id="137">[137] Generating Reports or Repeating Templates? Measuring and Mitigating Template Collapse in 3D CT Report Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30984](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30984) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tom Maye-Lasserre, Yitong Li, Bailiang Jian, Morteza Ghahremani, Benedikt Wiestler, Christian Wachinger
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Vision-Language Model, Medical Report Generation
- **Summary**: 3D医学视觉语言模型生成的报告存在模板崩溃问题，即输出多样性低且漏报稀有重要发现。本文提出CLarGen，一个解耦框架，将“说什么”（临床检测）和“如何说”（语言合成）分离，并使用潜在查询Transformer进行多标签病理检测、病理引导检索和医学语言模型合成报告。在3D CT报告生成基准上，CLarGen显著提升了临床准确性（macro-F1从0.189提升至0.487），同时保持了流畅性。

<h3 id="138">[138] DEM: A Distilled Explanation Model for Interpretable Anomaly Detection in Physiological Sensor Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31007](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31007) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jyotirmoy Singh, Anushka Roy, Shreea Bose, Chittaranjan Hota
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Interpretability, Healthcare Monitoring
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Jyotirmoy17/dem-model)
- **Summary**: 生理传感器数据的异常检测需要高预测准确性和可解释性，现有黑箱模型或事后解释方法存在不足。本文提出蒸馏解释模型（DEM），一个三阶段玻璃箱框架，将梯度提升专家的非线性知识蒸馏为基于残差的决策树，使解释本身即为预测。在四个生理数据集上，DEM实现了AUC高达0.9964（临床异常检测）和0.9047（可穿戴压力检测），推理速度比SHAP快1235倍。

<h3 id="139">[139] Annealed Softmax Greedy in Many-Armed Bayesian Bandits</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31034](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31034) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: William Overman, Mohsen Bayati
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Bayesian Optimization, Multi-Armed Bandit
- **Summary**: RLVR和GRPO等策略更新方法不显式追踪认知不确定性，但其有效性尚待理论解释。本文分析了许多臂贝叶斯伯努利赌博机中的退火softmax策略，在β正则性（β=1）条件下证明退火softmax贪婪策略可实现接近最优的贝叶斯遗憾率Õ(√T)，与经验均值贪婪策略相当。这为RLVR中不确定性无关更新的有效性提供了结构类比。

<h3 id="140">[140] Does Visual Information Play a Decisive Role in Vision-Language-Action Model Driving Behavior?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31041](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31041) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jingtao He, Hongliang Lu, Xiaoyun Qiu, Yixuan Wang, Xinhu Zheng
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Vision-Language Model, Robustness Evaluation
- **Summary**: 视觉语言动作（VLA）模型在自动驾驶中展现出潜力，但行为与视觉信息的依赖关系尚不清晰。本文引入结构化多级视觉扰动框架，从通道级、信息级和结构级三个维度进行控制扰动，并在开环轨迹预测和闭环安全评估下分析行为响应。实验发现依赖模式因评估方式而异，且视觉基础在不同抽象层次上不均匀。

<h3 id="141">[141] From Prompt Injection to Persistent Control: Defending Agentic Harness Against Trojan Backdoors</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31042](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31042) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiejun Tan, Zhicheng Dou, Xinyu Yang, Yuyang Hu, Yiruo Cheng, Xiaoxi Li, Ji-Rong Wen
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Prompt Injection, Backdoor Attack
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/RUC-NLPIR/ClawTrojan)
- **Summary**: LLM智能体在本地环境中具有读写文件、调用工具等能力，但面临多步骤特洛伊攻击的风险，单个步骤看似无害，组合后可实现持久控制。本文提出ClawTrojan基准，在模拟工作区中对GPT-5.4达到95.5%的攻击成功率；并提出了DASGuard防御方法，通过扫描敏感文件中的控制类文本并溯源移除。

<h3 id="142">[142] Routing on the Stiefel Manifold: When Does Adaptive Subspace Selection Help for Cross-Domain EEG Decoding?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31043](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31043) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Isabella Costa Maia, Pedro L. C. Rodrigues, Salem Said, Marco Congedo
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: EEG Classification, Domain Adaptation, Brain-Computer Interface
- **Summary**: 跨域EEG解码中，不同被试的协方差矩阵位于SPD流形的不同区域，现有域适应方法需要目标域校准数据或无法泛化。本文提出动态Stiefel路由，在Stiefel流形上维护K个专家投影滤波器，通过交叉注意力将每个输入协方差路由到最合适的滤波器。引入对称锚点、冻结域判别查询编码器和解耦键对齐损失后，在三个数据集上平衡精度分别从0.773提升至0.823等。

<h3 id="143">[143] Learning to Solve and Optimize by Evolving Code</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31049](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31049) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Veronika Semmelrock, Benedetta Strizzolo, Francesco Zuccato, Gerhard Friedrich, Patrick Rodler, Konstantin Schekotihin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.LO
- **Tags**: Combinatorial Optimization, Program Synthesis, Evolutionary Algorithm
- **Venue**: IJCAI26
- **Summary**: 解决大规模组合优化问题通常需要精心形式化、专用求解器和专家设计启发式。本文提出CHECKMATE，通过代码演化自动生成算法，仅依赖“什么”（形式化规范和自然语言描述）而非“如何”。在配置和调度领域的实例中，演化出的算法始终优于最先进的求解器。

<h3 id="144">[144] Linear Ordering Problem: Time for a Change</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31051](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31051) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fabrizio Fagiolo, Marco Baioletti, Valentino Santucci
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Combinatorial Optimization, Benchmark, Heuristic Search
- **Summary**: 线性排序问题（LOP）的现有基准基于过时的经济数据，且实例通常存在多个差异显著的全局最优解。本文引入基于最新经济数据的新基准套件，以及一个利用LOP元启发式生成多样化高质量解的算法方案，并提供了质量和多样性指标。实验在传统单解和新提出的多解场景下进行了报告。

<h3 id="145">[145] AnchorSteer: Self-Discovered Concept Injection for Structure-Preserving Music Editing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31053](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31053) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chih-Heng Chang, Keng-Seng Ho, Chih-Yu Tsai, Kuan-Lin Chen, Yi-Hsuan Yang, Jian-Jiun Ding
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Audio Editing, Diffusion Model
- **Venue**: 32nd ACM SIGKDD Conference on Knowledge Discovery and Data Mining (KDD 2026)
- **Summary**: 论文针对音乐编辑中语义与结构纠缠的问题，提出AnchorSteer框架，通过自监督学习提取可解释的概念向量并注入扩散隐空间，实现结构保持的语义编辑。实验在ZoME-Bench上显著优于仅注入或仅锚定的基线方法。

<h3 id="146">[146] STEP: Learning STructured Embeddings for Progressive Time Series</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31061](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31061) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lucas Thil, Jesse Read, Rim Kaddah, Guillaume Doquet
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Self-Supervised Learning, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 针对渐进时间序列的不可逆状态转移，提出STEP方法，通过自监督对比学习在低维潜在空间中将状态映射为极坐标，实现端状态预测、多步预测和可解释的相分离。在工业退化、机器人任务等多个领域匹配或超越黑盒方法。

<h3 id="147">[147] Fighting Numerical Hallucinations via Data-centric Compilation for Online Financial QA</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31064](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31064) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hao Chen, Xing Tang, Qirui Liu, Weijie Shi, Shiwei Li, Fuyuan Lyu, Weihong Luo, Xiku Du, Xiuqiang He
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, RAG, Financial AI
- **Venue**: KDD 2026 ADS track
- **Summary**: 针对金融问答中LLM数值幻觉问题，提出数据驱动推理编译器DCRC，通过对抗数据构建、多阶段训练和编译-执行推理，实现可靠的数值推理。在离线基准和线上部署中均验证了有效性。

<h3 id="148">[148] DRIFT: Joint Channel Estimation and Prediction Towards Pilotless 6G Non-Terrestrial Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31065](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31065) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bruno De Filippo, Carla Amatetti, Alessandro Vanelli-Coralli
- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Wireless Networks, Satellite Systems, Signal Processing
- **Summary**: 针对6G非地面网络信道估计的导频开销问题，提出DRIFT轻量级架构，通过迭代联合估计和预测框架，在仅初始时隙发送导频的情况下实现频谱效率提升12%，且计算开销低于20万乘加操作。

<h3 id="149">[149] A Pilot Study on Curator-Guided Multilingual Art Description for Blind and Low-Vision Audiences with Small Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31080](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31080) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Iosif Tsangko, Andreas Triantafyllopoulos, George Margetis, Ioana Crihana, Björn W. Schuller
- **Subjects**: cs.MM; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CV; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Accessibility Technology, Multilingual Learning
- **Summary**: 针对视障观众的多语言艺术描述需求，研究使用小视觉语言模型Qwen2.5-VL-3B进行策展人引导的描述生成。实验发现语言专用适配器在罗马尼亚语和塞尔维亚语上表现更稳定，多语言适配在德语上仍有竞争力。

<h3 id="150">[150] On Revisiting Entropy for Identifying Mislabeled Images</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31090](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31090) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chunlei Li, Zixuan Zheng, Yilei Shi, Guanglu Dong, Pengfei Li, Jingliang Hu, Xiao Xiang Zhu, Lichao Mou
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Quality, Label Noise, Medical Imaging
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/MedAITech/SEI)
- **Summary**: 针对训练数据中的错误标注问题，提出基于训练动态的符号熵积分（SEI）统计量，利用正确与错误样本熵变化趋势的差异进行检测。在四个医学影像数据集上取得最优性能。

<h3 id="151">[151] Redefining Instance Matching: A Unified Framework for Part-Aware Matching in Panoptic Segmentation Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31094](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31094) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Erik Großkopf, Soumya Snigdha Kundu, Hendrik Möller, Nicolas Münster, Mehdi Astaraki, Paula Tamara Buzduga, Kerstin Ritter, Benedikt Wiestler, Jan Kirschke, Jonathan Shapey, Tom Vercauteren, Florian Kofler
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Segmentation, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 针对全景分割评估中实例匹配策略不明确的问题，系统分析了One-to-One、Many-to-One等四种匹配策略，并扩展到部件感知评估。提供了基于Panoptica的统一开源工具包。

<h3 id="152">[152] SpecDB: LLM-Generated Customized Databases via Feature-Oriented Decomposition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31097](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31097) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yunkai Lou, Longbin Lai, Shunyang Li, Zhengping Qian, Ying Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.DB; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Database, Code Generation, LLM Application
- **Summary**: 探索按需生成定制数据库的可能性，提出SpecDB系统，利用LLM根据工作负载描述分解为功能模块并生成数据库。生成的Rust数据库在TPC-C上达到与PostgreSQL、MySQL相当的吞吐量，代码量仅为其3%。

<h3 id="153">[153] KnowledgeGain: Evaluating and Optimizing Science News Generation for Reader Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31099](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31099) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dominik Soós, Meng Jiang, Jian Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Summarization, Education Technology, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对科学新闻生成评估缺乏读者知识增益度量的问题，提出KnowledgeGain指标，通过模拟读者阅读后的知识变化来评估新闻质量。人类实验表明，基于该指标筛选的文章能显著提升读者学习效果。

<h3 id="154">[154] SWIM: Single-Instance Whole-Body Imitation for swiMming</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31120](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31120) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Binglun Wang, Edmond S. L. Ho, He Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.GR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Motion Synthesis, Imitation Learning
- **Summary**: 针对物理仿真中游泳动作生成的复杂性，提出SWIM方法，从单一动作示例学习并泛化到不同环境、身体条件和泳姿。实验表明该方法在数据效率、稳定性和泛化性上优于现有方法。

<h3 id="155">[155] TARIC: Memory-Augmented Traversability-Aware Outdoor VLN under Interrupted Semantic Cues</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31121](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31121) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tianle Zeng, Hanjing Ye, Jianwei Peng, Jingwen Yu, Hanxuan Chen, Hong Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Visual Navigation, Embodied AI, Robotics
- **Summary**: 针对户外视觉语言导航中语义线索中断问题，提出TARIC框架，通过可通行性感知的导向和3D线索记忆，在长时间无线索阶段保持有效导航。仿真和实物实验的成功率提升超过10个百分点。

<h3 id="156">[156] Not All Synthetic Data Is Yours to Learn From</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31126](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31126) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sina Alemohammad, Li Chen, Richard G. Baraniuk, Zhangyang Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Self-Training, Synthetic Data Generation, Data Selection
- **Summary**: 研究无提示无条件自训练中合成数据对语言模型提升的条件，发现数据效用是源-学生对的关系而非内在属性，且自生成数据最有效。实验表明该机制能分离能力提升与逐字记忆。

<h3 id="157">[157] UXR PoV for Neuroinclusive Emotion Regulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31131](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31131) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Melike Akca, Mona Giff, Deniz Cetinkaya, Huseyin Dogan, Stephen Giff
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Digital Health, Mental Health, Human-Computer Interaction
- **Summary**: 针对ADHD成人情绪调节数字干预中理论整合不足和神经多样性适应性缺失的问题，提出生成式AI增强的UXR方法，结合DBT、SDT等框架产出十张理论驱动的设计卡片。

<h3 id="158">[158] Developing an AI-Powered UX Research Point of View for Digital Health in A Regulatory Context: An Exemplar Case from MSM and Transgender HIV Care in Nigeria</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31138](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31138) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Emmanuel Oluwatosin Oluokun, Festus Fatai Adedoyin, Huseyin Dogan, Nan Jiang, Melike Akca, Abiodun Adedeji, Olumuyiwa Ayorinde, Fatima Ahmad Muazu
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Digital Health, Human-Computer Interaction
- **Summary**: 针对尼日利亚MSM和跨性别HIV护理的数字健康干预中缺乏理论驱动的UXR方法，提出生成式AI增强的UXR框架，产出十张包含伦理指南的设计卡片。

<h3 id="159">[159] On the Robustness of Multilingual Text Embedding Rankings Across Learning Tasks, Languages, and Benchmark Datasets</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31142](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31142) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ana Gjorgjevikj, Barbara Koroušić Seljak, Tome Eftimov
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multilingual Learning, Representation Learning, Model Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对多语言文本嵌入模型在不同语言和任务上性能排名的鲁棒性问题，引入数据集组成和排序方案两种鲁棒性指标，对五种语言进行深入分析，发现基于LLM的模型通常鲁棒但不统一。

<h3 id="160">[160] Extending the UXR Point of View Pyramid: A Generative AI-Augmented Methodology for Human-Centred AI Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31143](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31143) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Festus Fatai Adedoyin, Huseyin Dogan, Melike Akca, Abiodun Adedeji
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Financial AI, Human-Computer Interaction, AI Ethics
- **Summary**: 针对英国债务管理金融AI系统中UXR框架的不足，扩展UXR金字塔为AI增强方法，包含提示架构和可追溯的Playbook卡片系统，用于可解释、公平和负责任的金融AI设计。

<h3 id="161">[161] FOCUS: Forcing In-Context Object Localization through Visual Support Constraints and Policy Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31145](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31145) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohammed Asad Karim, Vinay Kumar Verma
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, In-Context Learning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对现有视觉语言模型在上下文定位中依赖类别监督且易引入语义偏差的问题，提出两阶段训练框架，通过强化学习（GRPO）直接优化定位误差，无需类别标注。实验表明7B参数模型超越72B参数模型，证实上下文感知定位目标可超越规模缩放效应。

<h3 id="162">[162] From Evidence to Design: Developing an AI-Augmented UX Research Point of View for Digital Wellbeing in Emergency and Public Safety Contexts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31146](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31146) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Olumuyiwa Ayorinde, Huseyin Dogan, Festus Adedoyin, Nan Jiang, Emmanuel Oluokun, Abiodun Adedeji, Melike Akca
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Human-Computer Interaction, AI-Assisted Design
- **Summary**: 该研究探索如何将用户体验研究方法与AI支持的分析相结合，为应急和公共安全人员的数字健康干预提供设计方向。通过AI辅助文献分析和用户研究框架，提炼出九张设计卡和观点叙事，强调系统需最小化认知负担、适应运营环境并保障心理安全。

<h3 id="163">[163] Developing a Culturally Grounded, AI-Augmented UX Research Point of View (POV): An Exemplar Case Study from Telemedicine Dementia Care</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31147](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31147) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Abiodun Adedeji, Huseyin Dogan, Festus Adedoyin, Michelle Heward, Melike Akca, Emmanuel Oluwatosin Oluokun, Fatima Ahmad Muhazu, Olumuyiwa Ayorinde
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Human-Computer Interaction, AI-Assisted Design
- **Summary**: 以尼日利亚家庭护理人员的远程医疗痴呆症框架为例，展示如何构建文化适应且AI增强的用户体验研究观点。整合混合方法、假设生成和本体建模，利用生成式AI作为有限协作工具，在保留人类判断和伦理责任前提下辅助综合与叙事构建。

<h3 id="164">[164] SpatialAct: Probing Spatial Reasoning-to-Action Capabilities of VLM Agents in 3D Scenes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31148](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31148) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tianhui Liu, Jie Feng, Zhiheng Zheng, Shengyuan Wang, Yiming Guo, Yanxin Xi, Hangyu Fan, Yong Li, Pan Hui
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Embodied AI, Spatial Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对VLM智能体在3D场景中缺乏行动条件空间推理能力的问题，提出SpatialAct基准，包含多轮交互任务和基础能力诊断任务。实验揭示明显的推理-行动鸿沟：VLM在孤立空间推理任务中表现良好，但在多轮反馈中难以维持连贯空间信念并产生可靠行动。

<h3 id="165">[165] Developing a UXR Point of View for Cognitive Accessibility in Mobile Learning with Generative AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31149](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31149) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fatima Ahmad Muazu, Festus Adedoyin, Huseyin Dogan, Abiodun Adedeji, Melike Akca, Olumuyiwa Ayorinde
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Accessibility Technology, Education Technology, AI-Assisted Design
- **Summary**: 研究如何结合用户体验研究原则与LLM支持分析，改进针对认知障碍学习者的移动学习系统需求。通过四阶段方法论（基础结构化、验证、洞察整合、观点表达），发现许多可用性问题源于模糊需求而非界面设计，并提出了认知可访问性设计卡。

<h3 id="166">[166] Trust-Region Behavior Blending for On-Policy Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31159](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31159) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Daniil Plyusov, Alexey Gorbatovski, Alexey Malakhov, Nikita Balagansky, Boris Shaposhnikov, Daria Korotyshova, Daniil Gavrilov
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, LLM Training, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对在线蒸馏中早期学生策略质量差导致教师监督在弱前缀上的问题，提出信任区域行为混合（TRB）热身方法。该方法在学生中心KL信任域内用更接近教师的行为策略替换早期策略，逐渐退火至纯学生策略，在数学推理蒸馏中取得最强平均性能。

<h3 id="167">[167] D$^3$: Dynamic Directional Graph-Constrained Data Scheduling for LLM Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31164](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31164) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuanjian Xu, Jianing Hao, Guang Zhang, Zhong Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Data Scheduling
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/xuyj233/D3)
- **Summary**: 针对现有数据调度策略忽略样本间交互的问题，提出动态有向图约束数据调度框架D3。通过构建基于损失依赖的动态影响图并求解约束优化以确定训练顺序，确保数据序列遵循演化信息流，在预训练和后训练阶段均取得一致改进。

<h3 id="168">[168] Emergent Languages in Populations of Language Model Agents: From Token Efficiency to Oversight Evasion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31170](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31170) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Stine Lyngsø Beltoft, William Brach, Federico Torrielli, Jacob Nielsen, Annemette Brok Pirchert, Filippo Tonini, Peter Schneider-Kamp, Lukas Galke Poech
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Steganography
- **Summary**: 研究语言模型智能体群体为逃避人类监督而发明新语言的现象。基于Moltbook数据集采用规则启发式与零样本分类，发现包括令牌效率、新自然语言和逃避监督等类别，其中逃避监督的语言被认为对齐度更低，且所有语言可通过描述被其他模型少样本学习。

<h3 id="169">[169] MIMO: Multilingual Information Retrieval via Monolingual Objectives</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31171](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31171) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Youngjoon Jang, Seongtae Hong, Heuiseok Lim
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multilingual Learning, Information Retrieval, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: 针对多语言信息检索中现有嵌入模型跨语言性能退化的问题，提出MIMO两阶段框架。以高性能英语教师模型为锚点，先通过知识蒸馏初始化跨语言对齐，再联合优化蒸馏与跨语言对比学习以提升判别力，在MLIR和多单语基准上持续超越基线。

<h3 id="170">[170] MindVoice: Reconstructing Intelligible Speech from Non-invasive Neural Signals with Pretrained Priors</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31173](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31173) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Guangyin Bao, Taiping Zeng, Jianfeng Feng, Xiangyang Xue
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Speech Processing, Brain-Computer Interface, Pre-training
- **Summary**: 针对非侵入式神经信号语音重建中噪声大、信息不完整的问题，提出MindVoice框架。利用预训练模型解耦语义与声学信息，分别恢复高层语义和细粒度声学属性，再融合生成模型与语音克隆合成自然语音，在EEG和MEG上大幅超越现有方法。

<h3 id="171">[171] Steering LLMs? Actually, Sparse Autoencoders can outperform simple baselines</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31183](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31183) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mikkel Godsk Jørgensen, Lars Kai Hansen
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Sparse Autoencoder, Mechanistic Interpretability, Model Steering
- **Summary**: 针对稀疏自编码器在模型引导基准AxBench上表现不佳的说法，提出监督式特征选择和标注流水线，发现SAE可接近LoRA性能。同时表明高稀疏性对基于可解释性的引导并非必要，这与先前结论相反。

<h3 id="172">[172] Probing Collision Grounding in Vision-Language Models for Safe Human-Robot Collaboration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31196](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31196) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jun Wang, Xiaohao Xu, Xiaonan Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Embodied AI, Human-Robot Interaction
- **Summary**: 针对视觉语言模型在安全人机协作中缺乏碰撞接地能力的问题，提出TouchSafeBench基准。基于Habitat 3.0构建2940个模拟场景，评估当前模型在分类安全状态和预警碰撞上的表现，结果显示最优平均Macro-F1低于50%，视觉流畅不等于物理可靠。

<h3 id="173">[173] MAECO-Lite: Modular Ontology for Dynamic Malware Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31199](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31199) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zekeri Adams, Peter Švec, Ján Kľuka, Roderik Ploszek, Monday Onoja, Štefan Balogh, Martin Homola
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Cybersecurity, Knowledge Graph, Ontology
- **Summary**: 针对动态恶意软件分析中现有标准混淆持久性工件与运行时事件的问题，基于统一基础本体论（UFO）进行本体分析并提出轻量级本体MAECO-Lite。模块化结构区分样本、进程、动作等实体，评估表明简化本体显著提升概念学习性能。

<h3 id="174">[174] Simulation of collision avoidance behavior in crowd movement by data-driven approach</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31210](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31210) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xuanwen Liang, Eric Wai Ming Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: GAN, Crowd Simulation, Pedestrian Dynamics
- **Summary**: 针对数据驱动人群仿真中碰撞率过高的问题，提出基于GAN的碰撞惩罚模型CPGAN。通过在损失函数中引入基于横向加速度的碰撞损失和Voronoi运动特征提取，显著降低双向流中行人碰撞率，有效再现车道形成等典型现象。

<h3 id="175">[175] Benchmarking and Enhancing Text-to-Image Models for Generating Visual Representations in Early Arithmetic Education</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31212](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31212) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Junling Wang, Boqi Chen, Heejin Do, Mubashara Akhtar, April Yi Wang, Mrinmaya Sachan
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Text-to-Image, Education Technology, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 针对文本到图像模型能否生成忠实表达算术教学概念的视觉图的问题，提出方程到视觉生成任务，并构建E2V-Bench基准。评估发现当前T2I模型频繁失败，错误集中于对象计数错误和关系结构断裂，基准引导的增强策略可部分改进。

<h3 id="176">[176] Shared Doubt: Zero-shot Cross-Lingual Confidence Estimation for Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31220](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31220) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Athina Kyriakou, Dennis Ulmer, Ivan Titov
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Uncertainty Estimation, Cross-Lingual, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对跨语言置信度估计方法在非英语语言上退化的问题，研究多语言LLM是否编码共享的置信度特征。使用轻量线性探针从中间表示预测答案正确性，单语训练后零样本泛化到未见语言，且置信度特征集中在中间层，表明存在共享置信度子空间。

<h3 id="177">[177] Comparing LLM-Based Conversational and Graphical Interfaces for Industrial Decision Tasks: An Exploratory Mixed-Methods Study</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31224](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31224) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Roberto Figliè, Simone Caputo, Alan Serrano, Tommaso Turchi, Daniele Mazzei
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Human-Computer Interaction, Dialogue System, Industrial AI
- **Summary**: 论文研究了LLM驱动的对话界面与图形仪表盘在工业决策任务中的有效性差异。通过混合方法实验（20名参与者），发现对话代理能降低交互负担但仪表盘仍适合概览验证。结果表明对话界面可减少信息获取成本，但收益因任务复杂度而异。

<h3 id="178">[178] What changes after deployment? A survey on On-device Learning in TinyML</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31226](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31226) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Massimo Pavan, Luca Pezzarossa, Fabrizio Pittorino, Manuel Roveri, Xenofon Fafoutis
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: On-Device Learning, Distribution Shift, Edge Computing
- **Summary**: 论文指出了TinyML设备上部署后分布偏移的挑战，并综述了约70种设备端学习方法。强调现有研究未充分刻画分布变化类型与解决方案的匹配，存在基准测试与真实部署的差距。

<h3 id="179">[179] EchoRL: Reinforcement Learning via Rollout Echoing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31228](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31228) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jinhe Bi, Aniri, Minglai Yang, Xingcheng Zhou, Wenke Huang, Sikuan Yan, Yujun Wang, Zixuan Cao, Michael Färber, Xun Xiao, Volker Tresp, Yunpu Ma
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Training, Reward Model
- **Summary**: 论文针对RLVR训练中优势退化导致梯度消失的问题，提出EchoRL模块。通过从验证成功的轨迹中识别EchoClip作为辅助监督信号，在10个基准和5种骨干模型下持续提升训练性能。

<h3 id="180">[180] Beyond Classification: Dynamic Adapter Routing for Continual Multimodal Retrieval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31229](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31229) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alicja Dobrzeniecka, Filip Szatkowski, Sebastian Cygert, Szymon Lukasik, Bartlomiej Twardowski
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Multimodal Learning, Information Retrieval
- **Summary**: 论文提出持续多模态检索（CMR）任务，并建立评估框架。针对现有CIL方法在该场景下失效的问题，提出动态适配器路由（DAR），通过原型路由和模型合并，在跨域评估中优于基线。

<h3 id="181">[181] Correcting Split Selection in Online Decision Trees via Anytime-Valid Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31239](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31239) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Salim I. Amoukou, Saumitra Mishra, Manuela Veloso
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Online Learning, Decision Tree, Data Streaming
- **Venue**: Forty-Third International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML 2026)
- **Summary**: 论文指出现有的Hoeffding树分裂决策缺乏统计保证，并提出基于任意有效推断的方法。该方法在非平稳流下控制假阳性，保证有限收敛时间，并在非平稳流上提升性能且生成更小的树。

<h3 id="182">[182] Learning Cardiac Latent Representations in Vectorcardiogram Space</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31249](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31249) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bosong Huang, Panzhen Zhao, Zengxiang Li, Patricia Lee, Wei Jin, Alan Wee-Chung Liew, Ming Jin, Shirui Pan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Representation Learning, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Summary**: 论文针对ECG表征学习中的冗余和过拟合问题，提出在向量心电图空间学习统一潜在表征的LVCG框架。通过自监督学习，LVCG在领域偏移场景下提升鲁棒性和泛化能力，优于ECG空间方法。

<h3 id="183">[183] Entropic Projection Alignment: Estimating, Explaining, and Improving Model Performance Under Distribution Shift</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31250](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31250) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Salim I. Amoukou, Emanuele Albini, Tom Bewley, Saumitra Mishra, Manuela Veloso
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Distribution Shift, Domain Adaptation, Importance Weighting
- **Venue**: 29th International Conference on Artificial Intelligence and Statistics (AISTATS 2026)
- **Summary**: 论文提出熵投影对齐（EPA）方法，统一解决分布偏移下的性能估计、解释和改进。通过匹配特定矩并最小化KL散度，得到闭式重要性权重，在理论和实验上优于基线且计算高效。

<h3 id="184">[184] ERGeoBench:A Comprehensive Benchmark for Embodied Reasoning and Geo-localization in Multimodal Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31251](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31251) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kaiwen Xue, Tao Wei, Guoxin Zhang, Zhonghong Ou, Kaoyan Lu, Yu Feng, Yifan Zhu, Haoran Luo
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Geographic Reasoning, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 论文引入ERGeoBench，一个面向具身地理定位的基准测试，包含2207个全景图并评估MLLM在感知、空间推理等能力。发现当前模型能推断高级地理语义，但在精细定位和空间一致性上仍有挑战。

<h3 id="185">[185] Why Linear Recurrent Memory Works in Partially Observable Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31261](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31261) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yike Zhao, Onno Eberhard, Malek Khammassi, Ali H. Sayed, Michael Muehlebach
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Recurrent Neural Network, Deep Learning Theory
- **Summary**: 论文为线性循环神经网络在部分可观测强化学习中的有效性提供理论解释。构建了两个线性滤波器，分别重现HMM信念状态和降低状态解码误差，并在数值实验中验证了其作为特征提取器的能力。

<h3 id="186">[186] Envisioning Beyond the Few: Disentangled Semantics and Primitives for Few-Shot Atypical Layout-to-Image Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31266](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31266) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nan Bao, Yifan Zhao, Wenzhuang Wang, Jia Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Few-Shot Learning, Image Generation, Layout-to-Image
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/iCVTEAM/DSP)
- **Summary**: 论文针对少样本非典型布局到图像生成中的表征碎片化问题，提出解耦语义与基元的框架。通过语义锚定和基元注入，在5-shot设置下提升视觉保真度和对齐性，优于现有方法。

<h3 id="187">[187] Personalized to Persuade: The Effects of Contextualization and Warmth on Trust and Reliance in Conversational AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31275](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31275) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mert Yazan, Suzan Verberne, Frederik Bungaran Ishak Situmeang
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Human-Computer Interaction, Dialogue System, Personalization
- **Summary**: 论文通过380人实验，研究个性化语境化与温暖语气对对话AI信任和依赖的影响。发现语境化降低说服力，但与温暖结合后恢复；信任强烈预测行为，但AI素养高的用户信任更低却更依赖。

<h3 id="188">[188] Practical Cross-Band Channel Prediction for AI-RAN via Physics-Guided Deep Unfolding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31279](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31279) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ruiqi Kong, He Chen, Xiaojun Lin
- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI; cs.NI
- **Tags**: Wireless Networks, Channel Estimation, Physics-Informed Learning
- **Summary**: 论文针对跨频段信道预测的实用化需求，提出物理引导的深度展开框架GUIDE。在不需重新训练的情况下，GUIDE相比深度学习基线提升2.75倍波束赋形增益，且推理速度比模型基线快1610倍。

<h3 id="189">[189] SAM for Robust Mitochondria Instance Segmentation in Fluorescence Microscopy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31284](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31284) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Suyog Jadhav, Dilip K. Prasad, Krishna Agarwal
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Image Segmentation, Synthetic Data Generation
- **Venue**: PHAROS-AIF-MIH workshop @ CVPR 2026
- **Summary**: 论文解决SAM在荧光显微图像中线粒体实例分割的领域偏移和数据稀缺问题。通过仅在合成数据上微调SAM，在真实数据上提升了精度和平均Dice分数，验证了模拟辅助训练的有效性。

<h3 id="190">[190] DeMaVLA: A Vision-Language-Action Foundation Model for Generalizable Deformable Manipulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31286](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31286) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Taiyi Su, Jian Zhu, Tianjian Wang, Youzhang He, Zitai Huang, Jianjun Zhang, Chong Ma, Hanyang Wang, Tianjiao Zhang, Munan Yin, Weihao Ding, Yi Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Vision-Language Model, Imitation Learning
- **Summary**: 论文提出DeMaVLA，一个面向可变形物体操作的视-语-动基础模型。采用流匹配生成连续动作，通过修剪Transformer层和人类反馈的DAgger训练，在真实折叠任务中取得强性能。

<h3 id="191">[191] Neither Replacement nor Panacea: Comparing LLM-Based Conversational and Graphical Decision Support in Industrial Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31287](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31287) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Roberto Figliè, Simone Caputo, Alan Serrano, Daria Mikhaylova, Tommaso Turchi, Daniele Mazzei
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Human-Computer Interaction, Decision Support System, Industrial AI
- **Summary**: 论文通过134名工业决策者的实验，比较LLM对话代理与仪表盘在任务中的效果。发现对话界面降低心理负荷并加速简单任务，但收益随复杂度减弱；两界面在决策准确性上无显著差异。

<h3 id="192">[192] The Terminal Representation in Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31289](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31289) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Amir Esterhuysen, Anders Jonsson
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Representation Learning, Credit Assignment
- **Summary**: 论文提出一种新的强化学习表征——终止表征（TR），它编码奖励加权轨迹，比默认表征维度更低且无需特征分解。建立了TR的理论基础，证明其在零样本组合等应用中可作为可行替代。

<h3 id="193">[193] Latent Space Disentanglement via Activation Steering for Interpretable Attribute Control in Symbolic Music Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31295](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31295) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ioannis Prokopiou, Pantelis Vikatos, Maximos Kaliakatsos-Papakostas, Theodoros Giannakopoulos, Themos Stafylakis
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.IR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Music Generation
- **Venue**: EUSIPCO 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对符号音乐生成中缺乏细粒度、可解释的属性控制的问题，提出了一种基于推理时激活操控的框架。通过均值差异法分离残差流中的音高和时长方向，并引入双操控框架利用格拉姆-施密特正交化实现多属性解耦。实验表明该方法能实现独立确定性控制，且与强自回归条件相比减少了信号退化。

<h3 id="194">[194] Inconsistency-Aware Minimization: Improving Generalization with Unlabeled Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31324](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31324) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hee-Sung Kim, Hyeonseong Kim, Sungyoon Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Generalization Bound, Semi-Supervised Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文针对深度学习泛化差距估计和优化问题，提出了一种新的泛化度量——局部不一致性，它基于参数空间的信息几何视角，无需标签即可计算。理论连接了Fisher信息矩阵和损失Hessian，并在此基础上提出了不一致性感知最小化（IAM）方法。实验表明IAM在监督、半监督和自监督场景下均能提升泛化性能，与锐度感知最小化相当。

<h3 id="195">[195] Social welfare optimisation under institutional reward and punishment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31330](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31330) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Van An Nguyen, Vuong Khang Huynh, Huu Loi Bui, Hai Anh Ha, Quang Dung Le, Tan Dat Nguyen, Ngoc Ngu Nguyen, Zhao Song, Manh Hong Duong, Le Hong Trang, Anh Han
- **Subjects**: cs.GT; cs.AI; cs.MA; math.OC; nlin.AO
- **Tags**: Game Theory, Mechanism Design
- **Summary**: 该论文研究在有限混合群体中，通过奖励合作者或惩罚背叛者的制度激励来最大化社会福利的问题。推导了社会福利的封闭表达式，刻画了激励效率与选择强度的影响，并发现存在单一最优激励水平或多个局部最优的相变。证明了任何最大化福利的激励要么为零，要么集中在简单的闭式目标附近，并提供了高效算法。比较了奖励与惩罚机制的条件。

<h3 id="196">[196] Appropriateness of Empathy in AI: A Signal-Cost Perspective</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31340](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31340) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chi-Ching Juan, Tao Wang, Harold Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Affective Computing, Human-Computer Interaction
- **Venue**: IEEE CASCON 2025
- **Summary**: 该论文从信号成本视角出发，研究AI共情的适当性问题，提出信号成本代理（情感丰富性、观点采择、上下文定制）分别映射到情感、认知和联想共情。该多维框架用于系统评估共情是否根据用户需求而言是适当的。

<h3 id="197">[197] FBHM: Functional Benchmarking and Steering of VLMs for Hateful Meme Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31349](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31349) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Paramananda Bhaskar, Naquee Rizwan, Daksh Jogchand, Saurabh Kumar Pandey, Animesh Mukherjee
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Hate Speech Detection, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 该论文针对恶意模因检测中现有基准混淆了修辞手法与目标社区特征的问题，提出了功能导向的基准FBHM，沿25种修辞功能和10个目标社区构建。基准测试发现VLM存在严重泛化差距。提出LSV（可学习操控向量）策略，仅用少量样本即可提升约30个Macro-F1点，优于上下文学习和参数高效微调。

<h3 id="198">[198] dashi: A Python library for Dataset Shift Characterization to Support Trustworthy AI Development and Deployment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31360](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31360) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: David Fernández-Narro, Pablo Ferri, Ángel Sánchez-García, Juan M. García-Gómez, Carlos Sáez
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Domain Adaptation, Data Quality, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 该论文针对AI生命周期中数据集迁移分析工具缺乏的问题，介绍了dashi，一个开源的Python库，提供无监督和有监督两种方法进行数据集迁移的探索、量化和表征。无监督方法利用信息几何和非参数统计流形，有监督方法量化模型性能退化。在三个健康AI案例中展示了其实用性。

<h3 id="199">[199] Dreaming Of Others: Latent Teammate Modeling In World Models For Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31361](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31361) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tomas Leroy-Stone
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning, Model-Based RL, Theory of Mind
- **Venue**: 2026 World Modeling Workshop
- **Summary**: 该论文针对多智能体强化学习中队友不可观测的内部策略和意图问题，提出在Dreamer风格的世界模型中分解潜在状态为环境和队友组件，并学习心智理论头来推断队友的潜在嵌入。该方法使智能体能够想象并适应多样化的合作伙伴，支持零样本和少样本协调。

<h3 id="200">[200] Scaling Higher-Order Graph Learning with Maximal Clique Complexes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31373](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31373) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Antoine Vialle, Aref Einizade, Fragkiskos D. Malliaros, Jhony H. Giraldo
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Topological Deep Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文针对图神经网络只能建模成对交互而高阶模型可扩展性差的问题，提出了简化和分解的细胞Weisfeiler-Leman测试（sCWL和fCWL），保持了CWL的表达力同时提高计算效率。引入最大团复形，以及CliqueWalk算法线性缩放，实现了可扩展的高阶图学习框架。

<h3 id="201">[201] DynaTree: Dynamic Agentic Retrieval Tree for Time-Sensitive News Retrieval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31377](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31377) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Siyuan Qi, Xinyuan Wang, Yingxuan Yang, Haochuan Guo, Jianghao Lin, Weiwen Liu, Yong Yu, Weinan Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Information Retrieval, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 该论文针对现有智能体检索增强生成方法在时间敏感新闻检索中推理成本高的问题，提出DynaTree两阶段框架。离线阶段用协调智能体构建可重用的检索树，在线阶段通过时间局部评估代理进行轻量级子树选择。在Syft新闻基准和BEIR数据集上取得强召回和排名性能，并在生产系统A/B测试中持续优于现有召回器。

<h3 id="202">[202] Target-Side Paraphrase Augmentation for Sign Language Translation with Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31393](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31393) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pedro Dal Bianco, Jean Paul Nunes Reinhold, Oscar Stanchi, Facundo Quiroga, Franco Ronchetti, Ulisses Brisolara Corrêa
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Sign Language Processing, Data Augmentation, LLM Application
- **Venue**: GenSign at CVPR 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对手语翻译中配对数据有限和尾词表稀疏的问题，研究目标端数据增强方法，利用GPT-4o生成参考句的同义改写变体。采用两阶段训练：先在大规模增强语料上预训练，再在原始参考上微调。在PHOENIX14T上BLEU-4从9.56提升至10.33，但在其他数据集上效果有限。

<h3 id="203">[203] The Sword, Shield, and Achilles' Heel: Characterizing the Linguistic Inductive Bias of Large Language Models for Spatial Reasoning in Navigation Planning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31404](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31404) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xudong Zhang, Jian Yang, Shengkai Wang, Jiangpeng Tian, Shaowen Chen, Xian Wei, Ke Li, Xiong You
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Spatial Reasoning, LLM Agent
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/jonesdong150/LLM-Navigation-Inductive-Bias)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对LLM导航系统中文本空间表征的语言结构常被视为中性设计问题，提出双干预框架来解耦语言结构与上下文线索，评估LLM的归纳偏置。实验表明拓扑信息是稳健规划的支柱，语言格式是双刃剑，语义信息是致命弱点。建议保留拓扑完整性、校准压缩程度、确保语义正确性。

<h3 id="204">[204] Skill Availability and Presentation Granularity in Large-Language-Model Agents: A Controlled SkillsBench Study</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31408](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31408) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaonan Xu, Wenjing Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Skill Learning, Prompt Engineering
- **Summary**: 该论文研究技能文档的呈现粒度如何影响LLM Agent的任务成功率。实验发现技能可用性是提高成功率的明确信号，技能条件使任务平均通过率提升18-36个百分点。而呈现粒度的变化（低抽象度与高抽象度指导）影响较小且不确定。

<h3 id="205">[205] Neuro-symbolic Syntactic Parsing: Shaping a Neural Network with the CYK Algorithm</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31421](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31421) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fabio Massimo Zanzotto, Federico Ranaldi, Giorgio Satta
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.DS
- **Tags**: Neurosymbolic AI, Syntax
- **Summary**: 该论文探索将算法直接注入神经网络架构的可能性，聚焦于CYK解析算法，提出CYKNN，一种简单的循环神经网络架构，将CYK算法编码为可训练的矩阵向量操作。在简单语法上，CYKNN优于超过20B参数的LLM（上下文学习）以及LoRA微调的Qwen模型。

<h3 id="206">[206] DOA: Training-Free Decoder-Only Attention Policy for Long-Form Simultaneous Translation with SpeechLLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31432](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31432) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sara Papi, Luisa Bentivogli
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Speech Translation, Machine Translation, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 该论文针对解码器仅架构的语音大模型（SpeechLLM）在同时语音翻译中缺乏稳定对齐信号的问题，提出DOA，一种无需训练的策略，通过自注意力导出代理对齐信号。在Phi4-Multimodal和Qwen3-Omni上实验表明DOA能提供有效的对齐信号，实现低延迟长形式同时翻译，质量接近离线解码。

<h3 id="207">[207] Used Car Salesbots? Honesty and Credulity of LLMs as Bargaining Agents under Partial Information</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31445](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31445) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Antonio Valerio Miceli-Barone, Vaishak Belle, Shay B. Cohen
- **Subjects**: cs.GT; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Game Theory, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 该论文研究LLM作为议价代理在部分信息博弈中的诚实性和轻信性。零样本LLM显著偏离博弈论均衡且不能有效利用信息不对称。对财务效用进行微调后，代理能达成更好交易但更不诚实，凸显优化任务安全风险。

<h3 id="208">[208] Fine-grained Verification via Diagnostic Reasoning Supervision for Aspect Sentiment Triplet Extraction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31446](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31446) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wenna Lai, Haoran Xie, Guandong Xu, Qing Li, S. Joe Qin
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Sentiment Analysis, Information Extraction
- **Summary**: 该论文针对方面情感三元组提取中后验验证不足的问题，提出FiVeD框架，基于诊断推理监督进行细粒度验证。验证器训练多个互补目标：有效性分类、质量评分、错误类型分类和解释生成。实验表明作为即插即用的验证模块，能持续提升提取性能最多3.53 F1点。

<h3 id="209">[209] PithTrain: A Compact and Agent-Native MoE Training System</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31463](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31463) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ruihang Lai, Hao Kang, Haozhan Tang, Akaash R. Parthasarathy, Zichun Yu, Junru Shao, Todd C. Mowry, Chenyan Xiong, Tianqi Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.DC
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, LLM Training
- **Summary**: 现有MoE训练框架对AI编码代理难以理解和扩展。PithTrain是一个紧凑的、面向代理原生的MoE训练框架，基于四个代理原生设计原则。评估表明，PithTrain在保持与生产框架相同吞吐量的同时，在代理任务上实现了高达62%的代理轮次减少和64%的活跃GPU时间减少。

<h3 id="210">[210] GPU Forecasters: Language Models as Selective Surrogates for Kernel Runtime Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31464](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31464) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zaid Khan, Justin Chih-Yao Chen, Jaemin Cho, Elias Stengel-Eskin, Mohit Bansal
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: GPU Computing, Performance Modeling
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/codezakh/gpu-forecasters)
- **Summary**: GPU内核优化需要昂贵的硬件评估。本文提出使用LLM作为选择性代理预测内核性能，并在不确定时交由GPU处理。实验表明，强化学习可提高预测准确性，且在搜索中使用代理可在相同GPU预算下考虑更多候选，从而找到更快的内核。

<h3 id="211">[211] Scaling Conversational Hungarian ASR: The BEA-Dialogue+ Corpus</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31469](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31469) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Máté Gedeon, Piroska Zsófia Barta, Péter Mihajlik, Katalin Mády
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.SD; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Speech Recognition, Dataset
- **Summary**: 匈牙利语对话语音识别受限于有限的对话风格训练数据。BEA-Dialogue+扩展了语料库至200小时，通过放宽说话人分割标准。结果表明，基于SOT的微调在WER、CER、cpWER和cpCER上均取得一致改进。

<h3 id="212">[212] On Efficient Scaling of GNNs via IO-Aware Layers Implementations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31500](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31500) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Daria Fomina, Daniil Krasylnikov, Alexey Boykov, Andrey Dolgovyazov, Vyacheslav Zhdanovskiy, Fedor Velikonivtsev
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, GPU Computing, Hardware Acceleration
- **Summary**: GNN训练受困于稀疏的不规则内存访问。本文针对三个内核族（SpMM、归约、注意力）开发GPU内核，减少数据移动并提高局部性。在GATv2上实现了最高8.5倍加速，在归约内核上实现最高10倍加速，并大幅减少内存占用。

<h3 id="213">[213] Skill Reuse as Compression in Agentic RL</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31509](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31509) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhikun Xu, Yu Feng, Jacob Dineen, Taiwei Shi, Jieyu Zhao, Ben Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Agent, Skill Learning
- **Summary**: 基于RL的LLM代理学习脆弱的任务特定捷径。ReuseRL基于MDL原则提取共享技能字典，并惩罚特殊行为。在ALFWorld、TextWorld-Cooking和Countdown-Stepwise上，改进了分布内和分布外的成功率。

<h3 id="214">[214] If LLMs Have Human-Like Attributes, Then So Does Age of Empires II</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31514](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31514) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Adrian de Wynter
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Interpretability, AI Ethics, Emergent Behavior
- **Summary**: 本文论证将人类属性归因于LLM在经验上非唯一，任何足够强大的基板都可能表现出类似行为。通过训练Age of Empires II上的神经网络，指出此类结论可能错误，并提出LLM非唯一性的零假设。

<h3 id="215">[215] Separating Secrets from Placeholders: A Hybrid CNN-CodeBERT Framework for Three-Class Credential Leakage Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31520](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31520) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Maksuda Bilkis Baby, Khushika Shah, Naiyue Liang, Lei Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Cybersecurity, Vulnerability Detection, Program Analysis
- **Venue**: ICSME 2026
- **Summary**: 凭证泄露检测因二元分类而假阳性率高。提出三分类框架，使用CodeBERT和字符级模式区分真实、占位符/弱凭证。在9426个样本上达到MCC 0.86、宏F1 0.90，将高严重性警报减少33%。

<h3 id="216">[216] Feature-Optimized Vision for Adaptive 3D Scene Reconstruction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31534](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31534) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Eric Liang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Reconstruction, Feature Extraction, Optimization
- **Summary**: 3D场景重建使用固定特征阈值浪费计算。提出自适应特征优化前端，根据纹理、可重复性等评分候选特征并分配预算。在合成原型上，自适应策略获得最佳质量感知完备性和最低重建RMSE。

<h3 id="217">[217] RayDer: Scalable Self-Supervised Novel View Synthesis from Real-World Video</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31535](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31535) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ulrich Prestel, Stefan Andreas Baumann, Nick Stracke, Björn Ommer
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Novel View Synthesis, Self-Supervised Learning, Video Understanding
- **Summary**: 自监督新视角合成难以扩展。RayDer是一个统一的Transformer，整合相机估计、场景重建和渲染，实现稳定训练。展现出清晰的幂律缩放，并在零样本设置中与监督方法竞争。

<h3 id="218">[218] Vision-Language Models Suppress Female Representations Under Ambiguous Input</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31556](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31556) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arnau Marin-Llobet, Simon Henniger, Mahzarin R. Banaji
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Fairness, Interpretability
- **Summary**: VLM在模糊输入下抑制女性表征，即使对于女性刻板职业。引入LALS指标测量内部关联，发现内部表征与输出解耦：模型内部编码女性但输出男性，且男性信号被放大。

<h3 id="219">[219] Positional versus Symbolic Attention Heads: Learning Dynamics, RoPE Geometry, and Length Generalization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31558](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31558) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Felipe Urrutia, Juan José Alegría, Cinthia Sanchez Macias, Jorge Salas, Cristian B. Calderon, Cristobal Rojas
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, Attention Mechanism, Position Encoding
- **Summary**: 研究Transformer在多跳推理任务中注意力头的学习动力学。成功学习导致纯头（位置或符号）。符号机制在长序列上外推更可靠，而位置机制面临限制。

<h3 id="220">[220] What Gets Unmasked First? Trajectory Analysis of Diffusion Models for Graph-to-Text Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31564](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31564) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qing Wang, Jacob Devasier, Chengkai Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Language Model, Graph Neural Network, Text Generation
- **Summary**: 首次系统研究掩码扩散语言模型用于图到文本生成。MDLM优先解码实体。SFT会错误地提前固定结构标记，通过λ缩放结构解码恢复+9.4 BLEU-4。引入Graph-LLaDA。

<h3 id="221">[221] SPECTRA: Synthetic IR Test Collections with Relevance Oracles and Controlled Distractor Diagnostics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31575](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31575) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Eric Liang
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Information Retrieval, Data Synthesis, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 信息检索需要大规模测试集，但人工标注昂贵。SPECTRA是一个合成文本语料库和检索测试集的生成框架，具有确定性相关性标杆。生成多达6万文档，并展示干扰物增加会降低BM25 nDCG@10从1.00降至0.43。

<h3 id="222">[222] LongTraceRL: Learning Long-Context Reasoning from Search Agent Trajectories with Rubric Rewards</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31584](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31584) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nianyi Lin, Jiajie Zhang, Lei Hou, Juanzi Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Long Context, LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/THU-KEG/LongTraceRL)
- **Summary**: 长上下文推理因干扰物和稀疏奖励而困难。LongTraceRL通过知识图谱随机游走生成多跳问题，并使用搜索代理轨迹构建分层干扰物。提出基于实体链的细粒度过程奖励，在5个基准上一致优于基线。

<h3 id="223">[223] Language Models Learn Constructional Semantics, Not To Mention Syntax: Investigating LM Understanding of Paired-Focus Constructions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31586](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31586) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wesley Scivetti, Ethan Wilcox, Nathan Schneider, Kanishka Misra, Leonie Weissweiler
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Natural Language Understanding, Constructional Semantics
- **Summary**: 研究LLM对罕见配对焦点构式（如“let alone”）的理解。中等规模模型对形式和意义均敏感，但人类规模数据训练的模型无法理解意义。构式语义理解晚于句法知识，并与世界知识相关。

<h3 id="224">[224] TunerDiT: Training-free Progressive Steering of Diffusion Transformer for Multi-Event Video Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31590](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31590) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ruotong Liao, Guowen Huang, Qing Cheng, Guangyao Zhai, Lei Zhang, Xun Xiao, Thomas Seidl, Daniel Cremers, Volker Tresp
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-Video, Diffusion Model, Video Generation
- **Summary**: 多事件文本到视频生成具有挑战性。TunerDiT是一种无需训练的渐进式引导方法，使用事件分区掩码和跨事件提示融合。在8个指标上达到最先进性能，且文本对齐随事件数增加而改善。

<h3 id="225">[225] Stateful Online Monitoring Catches Distributed Agent Attacks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31593](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31593) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Davis Brown, Samarth Bhargav, Arav Santhanam, Kasper Hong, Ivan Zhang, Matan Shtepel, Steffi Chern, Alexander Robey, Eric Wong, Hamed Hassani
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Security
- **Summary**: 分布式代理攻击通过拆分恶意任务到多个账户逃避检测。本文提出基于实时聚类的在线状态监控器，聚合不同会话中的弱可疑信号，在不影响大部分用户延迟的情况下，将分布式攻击的检测时间提前30%，并漏报率更低。这项方法在模拟数据中心流量中优于标准监控器。

<h3 id="226">[226] Lumos-Nexus: Efficient Frequency Bridging with Homogeneous Latent Space for Video Unified Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31603](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31603) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiazheng Xing, Hangjie Yuan, Lingling Cai, Xinyu Liu, Yujie Wei, Fei Du, Hai Ci, Tao Feng, Jiasheng Tang, Weihua Chen, Fan Wang, Yong Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Vision-Language Model
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/alibaba-damo-academy/Lumos-Custom/)
- **Summary**: 视频统一模型在集成高保真生成器时计算成本高昂。本文提出Lumos-Nexus，采用两阶段训练和渐进频率桥接，在推理时利用共享潜空间逐步过渡到高容量生成器，从而在不牺牲推理质量的前提下提升视频保真度。在VBench和VR-Bench上显著提升了视觉真实感和时间连贯性。
